Clustering Benchmark - Simplified Sequential Version\n// \n// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters\n// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point\n// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n    \n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n    \n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n        \n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n        \n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n            \n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            \n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n// Returns -1 if no such point exists\nint findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& in_cluster,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold,\n                     const int point_count) {\n    int closest_point = -1;\n    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n    \n    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate\n    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {\n        // Skip if already clustered or already in this cluster\n        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;\n        \n        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members\n        double max_dist = 0.0;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = cluster_members[i];\n            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);\n            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);\n        }\n        \n        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best\n        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {\n            min_diameter = max_dist;\n            closest_point = candidate;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return closest_point;\n}\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                              const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                              const double threshold,\n                              const int point_count,\n                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\n    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    \n    // Add seed point\n    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;\n    members.push_back(seed_point);\n    \n    // Iteratively add closest points\n    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {\n        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, \n                                             threshold, point_count);\n        \n        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added\n        \n        in_cluster[closest] = true;\n        members.push_back(closest);\n    }\n    \n    // Copy members if requested\n    if (cluster_members) {\n        *cluster_members = members;\n    }\n    \n    return static_cast<int>(members.size());\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    \n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n    \n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        int best_seed = -1;\n        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;\n        \n        // Try each unclustered point as a seed\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {\n            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\n            if (clustered[seed]) continue;\n            \n            std::vector<int> candidate_members;\n            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, \n                                                       threshold, N, \n                                                       &candidate_members);\n            \n            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {\n                max_cardinality = cardinality;\n                best_seed = seed;\n                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;\n            }\n        }\n        \n        // If we found a cluster, add it\n        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;\n            clusters.push_back(cluster);\n            \n            // Mark all members as clustered\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;\n            }\n            \n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n            unclustered_indices.erase(\n                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),\n                unclustered_indices.end()\n            );\n        } else {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n    \n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n    \n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], \n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n        \n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\", \n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n        \n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\", \n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];\n            if (membership[member] >= 0) {\n                printf(\"ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);\n    \n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v Fine. But is a barrier needed before critical to ensure all threads' global writes? Each thread individually merges. Since critical serializes, final after parallel barrier correct. nowait just lets threads finish for loop and merge; all for iterations complete before end-of-parallel implicit barrier for each thread (each thread finished its own). Even though one thread might merge before another finishes its iterations, later merge still happens. Fine.\n\nBut wait a serious issue: `ThreadWorkspace& ws = workspaces[omp_get_thread_num()]` then `ws.resetBest()` before `#pragma omp for`. Then for loop iterations. After a thread finishes its chunk (nowait) it enters critical and merges. However while a thread is inside critical merging, could another thread still be using the *same* ws? No, ws is per thread. All good.\n\nNow there is a data race on `max_cardinality`, `best_seed`, `best_index`, `best_cluster_members`? They're only accessed inside critical during parallel. But initial writes before parallel (main thread). Critical does not by itself guarantee visibility to main after region? Implicit barrier flushes memory. adding the closest point\\n5: // that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.\\n6: \\n7: #include <algorithm>\\n8: #include <chrono>\\n9: #include <cmath>\\n10: #include <cstdio>\\n11: generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\\n34:     auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\\n35:     \\n36:     const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\\n37:     int count = 0;\\n38:     \\n39:     while (count < N) {\\n40:         // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\\n45:         int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\\n46:         \\n47:         // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\\n48:         if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\\n49:             group_cnt = N - count;\\n50:         }\\n51:         \\n52:         while (group_cnt > 0) {\\n53:             const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\\n54:             const double r = frand() * R;\\n55:             const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\\n56:             const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\\n57:             const double x = cntr_x + dx;\\n58:             const double y = cntr_y + dy;\\n59:             \\n60:             if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\\n61:                 continue;\\n62:             }\\n63:             \\n64:             points[count] = {x, y};\\n65:             count++;\\n66:             group_cnt--;\\n67:         }\\n68:     }\\n69: }\\n70: \\n71: // Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\\n72: inline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\\n73:     double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\\n74:     double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\\n75:     return                const std::vector<Point>& points,\\n84:                      const double threshold,\\n85:                      const int point_count) {\\n86:     int closest_point = -1;\\n87: {\\n91:         // Skip if already clustered or already in this cluster\\n92:         if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;\\n93:         \\n94:         // Calculate the maximum cluster_members[i];\\n98:             const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);\\n99:             max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);\\n100:         }\\n101:         \\n102:         // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best\\n103:         if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {\\n104:             min_diameter = unclustered_indices.end()\\n200:             );\\n201:         } else {\\n202:             // No more clusters can be formed\\n203:             break;\\n204:         }\\n205:     }\\n206:     \\n207:           const std::vector<Point>& points,\\n213:                      const double threshold) {\\n214:     bool valid = true;\\n215:     \\n216:     printf(\\\"Validating clusters:\\\\n\\\");\\n217: Structure to represent a point in 2D space\\nstruct Point {\\n    double x, y;\\n};\\n\\n// Structure to represent a cluster\\nstruct Cluster {\\n    std::vector<int> members;\\n    int need\\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\\n            group_cnt = N - count;\\n        }\\n        \\n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : double x = cntr_x + dx;\\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\\n            \\n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\\n                continue;\\n            }\\n            \\n            points[count] = {x, y};\\n            count++;\\n            group_cnt--;\\n        }\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\\ninline double \\n        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added\\n        \\n        in_cluster[closest] = true;\\n        members.push_back(closest);\\n    }\\n    \\n    // Copy members if   }\\n    \\n    // Check for duplicate memberships\\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < This is the same value the\n111: // original implementation recomputed from scratch on every iteration, so the\n112: // resulting cluster is identical while being asymptotically cheaper.\n113: //\n114: // Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster; Reusing it avoids allocating vectors for every\n// candidate seed and lets the membership stamps be reset in O(1).\nstruct ThreadWorkspace {\n    std::vector<int> stamp;\n    std::vector<double> max_dist;\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    std::vector<int> best_members;\n    int generation = 0;\n    int best_cardinality = -1;\n    int best_seed = -1;\n    size_t best_index = 0;\n\n    void init(const int n) {\n        stamp.assign(n, 0);\n        max_dist.resize(n);\n        members.reserve(n);\n        best_members.reserve(n);\n    }\n\n    void resetBest() {\n        best_cardinality = -1;\n        best_seed = -1;\n        best_index = std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max();\n    }\n};\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point.\n//\n// The maximum distance from every remaining candidate to the cluster is\n// maintained incrementally: when a point joins the cluster only the distance\n// to that new member has to be considered.  This is the same value the\n// original implementation recomputed from scratch on every iteration, so the\n// resulting cluster is identical while being asymptotically cheaper.\n//\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster; members are left in\n// ws.members.\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                             const std::vector<char>& clustered,\n                             const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                             const double threshold,\n                             const int point_count,\n                             ThreadWorkspace& ws) {\n    if (++ws.generation == std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) {\n        std::fill(ws.stamp.begin(), ws.stamp.end(), 0);\n        ws.generation = 1;\n    }\n    const int gen = ws.generation;\n\n    int* const stamp = ws.stamp.data();\n    double* const max_dist = ws.max_dist.data();\n    const Point* const pts = points.data();\n    std::vector<int>& members = ws.members;\n\n    members.clear();\n\n    // Add seed point\n    stamp[seed_point] = gen;\n    members.push_back(seed_point);\n\n    // Initial maximum distance of each candidate is its distance to the seed.\n    const double seed_x = pts[seed_point].x;\n    const double seed_y = pts[seed_point].y;\n    for (int c = 0; c < point_count; ++c) {\n        const double dx = pts[c].x - seed_x;\n        const double dy = pts[c].y - seed_y;\n        max_dist[c] = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n    }\n\n    // Iteratively add closest points\n    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {\n        int closest = -1;\n        double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n\n        for (int c = 0; c < point_count; ++c) {\n            if (clustered[c] || stamp[c] == gen) continue;\n            const double d = max_dist[c];\n            if (d < threshold && d < min_diameter) {\n                min_diameter = d;\n                closest = c;\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (closest < 0) break;
